करा . 832 -- - केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रसंत होता है कि लोकहि । में । करना प्रावश्यक है कि गजगत राज्य में कोहला से पंजाब राम में 
भटिण्डा नफ राजस्थान और हरियाणा में होकर पेट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिए इंडियन प्रापन कार्पोरेशन लिमिटेड द्वाराप हन far जा . 


और मा प्रतीत होता है कि उन पापलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजनो के लिए इस अधिसूचना से उपाबद्ध अनमूची में वर्णित भूमि के उपयोग के अधिकार 
का प्रर्जन करना प्रावश्यक है , अतः अब केन्द्रीय सरकार , पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जT ) अधिनियम , 1967 
11982 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदान कियों का प्रयोग करते हुए , उनमें उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन करने के अपने प्राणय की 
घोषणा करती है । 


उप मन सूची में वर्णित भूमि मे हिल बद कोई व्यक्ति, राजपन में यथा प्रकाशिन इम अधिपूचना की प्रतियो जनता को उपलब्ध करा दिए जाने की 

से 31 दिन के भीतर उनमें उपयोग के अधिकार का प्रर्जन या भूमि में पाइप लाइन बिछाने में प्रापलि लिखित रूप में श्री कृपाल सिंह, भमि अर्जन. 
अधिकारी और सक्षम प्राधिकारी, इंडियन भायल कापोरेशन लिमिटेड , कांडला भटिंडा पाइपन इन परियोजना, मेक्टर 17- पी , 1 मी पो 178 अंडीगन को कर 
मकेगा ।

MINISTRY OF PETROLEUM AND CHEMICALS 


(Department of Petroleuin and NaturalGas) 

New Delhi, the 13th March , 1991 
S . O . 832 :- Whereas it appears to the Central Government that it is necessary in the public 
interest that for the transport of Petroleum from Kandla in the State ofGujarat to Bhatinda in the State of 
Punjab , via Rajasthan and Haryana , Pipelines should be laid by the Indian Oil Corporation Limited ; 

And whereas it appears that for the purpose of laying such pipelines it is necessary to acquire the right 
of user in the land described in the Schedule annexed to this notification ; 

Now , therefore , in exercise of the powers conferred by sub -section (1 ) of section 3 of the Petroleum 
and Minerals Pipelines (Acquisition of Right of User in Land ) Act, 1962 (50 of 1962 ), the Central Govern 
ment hereby declares its intention to acquire the right of user therein . 

Any person interested in the land described in the said schedule may within 21 days from the date on 
which the copies of this notification , as published in the Gazette of India , are made available to the general 
public , object in writing to the acquisition of the right of user therein or laying of the pipeline under the 
land to Shri Kirpal Singh , Land acquisition Officer and Competent Authority , Indian Oil Corporation Ltd ., 
Kandla - Bhatinda Pipeline Project, Sector 37 - C , SCO 178 Chandigarh .

का० अ .0813.-- -केन्द्रीय मरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है कि लोक हित मे ऐसा करना प्रावश्यक है कि गुजरात राज्य मे कामना से पंजाब राज्य में भटिया 
नक राजस्थान और हरियाणा में होकर पैट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिए इलियन बोयल कॉर्पोरेशन लिमिटेड द्वारा पाइपलाइन बिछाई जाए . . 

और ऐसा प्रतीत होता है कि उक्त पाइप लाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजनो के लिए इस अधिसूचना मे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में घणित भूमि के उपयोग के प्रभार 
का प्रर्जन करना प्रावश्यक है । 

अन , प्रब , मोदीय सरकार, पैट्रोलियम और खनिम पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का पर्जन ) अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50) फी 
धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त पाक्लियो का प्रयोग करते हुए , उनमे उपयोग के अधिकार का प्रर्जन करने में अपने प्राशय की घोषणाकती है । 

उन अनमूची में वर्णित भूमि में हिसबब कोई व्यक्ति , गजपत्र में यथा प्रकाशित इम अधिसूचना की प्रतियां जनना को उपलब्ध करा दिए जाने की 
नागेन में 21 दिन के भीतर उनमें उपयोग के अधिकार का प्रर्जन या भूमि में पाइपलाइन बिछाने में आपत्ति लिखित रूप में श्री कृपाल मिह, भमि अर्जन 
मधिकारी और मक्षम प्राधिकारी, इमियन ऑयल कॉर्पोरेशन लिमिटेड, कौवना-भटिया पाइप लाइन परियोजना, सेक्टर-37- सी , स मी पो 189 चंडीगढ़ को कर 
सकेगा ।

and Minerals Pipelins (Acquisition of Right of User in Lan ) Act, 1962 (50 of 1962),the CentralGovernment 
hereby declares its intention to acquire the right of user therein . 

Any pzrson interested in the land described in the said schedult may within 21 days from the date 
on which the copics of this notification , as publishe:l in the Gazette of Inlio , arc made availabk to the 
general public , objcct in writing to the aoquisition of th : right of user theroin or laying of the pipeline

का . आ . 834.-- - केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है कि लोक हित में ऐसा करना प्रारमक है कि गुजरात राज्य में मारा 
पंजाब राज्य में भटिम्डा तक राजस्थान और हरियाणा में होकर पेट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिए इंटिमम मीपल कार्पोरेशन लिमिटेर नारा पारसलान 
बिछाई जाए। 

मौर ऐसा प्रतीत होता है कि उक्त पाइप लाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिए इम मसूिचना से उपाबढ मनुसूची में वर्णित भमि के उपयोग 
के अधिकार का मजन करमा प्रावश्यक है अत . अब , केन्द्रीय सरकार, पंद्रोलियम पार पनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का मन 
अधिनियम , 1961 ( 1962 का 50) की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत शक्तियो का प्रयोग करते हुए, उममे उपयोग के अधिकार 
का भजन करने के अपने माशय की घोषणा करती है । 

उक्त मनसूची मे गणित भूमि में हितबद कोई व्यक्ति रागपत्र मे गयाप्रकाशित इम अधिसूचना की प्रसियर्या जनता को उपलब्ध करा 
दिए जाने की नारीख से 21 दिन के भीतर उनम उपयोग के अधिकार का भजन या भूमि में पाइपलाइन बिछाने में भापति लिखित माप 
में भो कपाल मिह भूमि पर्षन अधिकारी नार मक्षम प्राधिकाग , इंडियन प्रायल मार्पोरेशन लिमिटेड कांस्ना- भटिडा पापसाइम परियोजना

S . O . 

834. — Whereas it appears to the Central Government that it is necessary in the 
public interest that for the transport of Petroleum from Kandla in the State of Gujarat t 
State of Punjab , via Rajasthan and Haryana, Pipelines should be laid by the Indian Oil Corporation 
Limited ; 

And whereas it appears that for the purpose of laying such pipelines it is necessary to acquire the 
right of user in the land described in the Schedule annexed to this notification ; 


Now , therefore , in exercise of the powers conferred by sub - section ( 1) of section 3 of the Petroleum and 
Mincrals Pipelines (Acquisition of Right of User in Land ) Act, 1962 (50 of 1962),the Central Government 
hereby declares its intention to acquire the right of user therein . 


Any person interested in the land described in the said schedule may within 21 days from the datc on 
which the copies of this notification , as published in the Gazette of India ,are made available to the general 
public, objeot in writing to the acquisition of the right of user therin or laying of the pipeline under the 
land to Shri Kirpal Singh , Land Acquisition Officer and Competent Authority , lodian Oil Corporation 
Ltd ., Kandla - Bhatinda Pipeline Project, Sector 37 - C , SCO 178 Chandigarh .

[ No . R - 31015/ 2/ 90 - ORI ] 
का प्रा . 835 : -~-केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है कि लोकहित में ऐमा करना मावश्यक है कि गुजरात राज्य में काला से पजाब राज्य में 
भटिम्प तक राजस्थान और हरियाणा से होकर पेट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिए इंडियन आयल कारपोरेशन लिमिटेड द्वारा पाइपलाइन बिछाई जाए ; 

और ऐमा प्रतीत होता है कि उन पाइपलाइन न बिछाने के प्रयोजनो के लिए इस अधिसूचना में उपाबद्ध अनमूभी मे चणित भूमि के उपयोग के अधिकार 
का प्रनन करना प्रावश्यक है । प्रन अब, केन्द्रीय सरकार , पेट्रोलियम और निम पाइपलाइन ( भूमि म उपयोग का अधिकार का अर्जन ) अधिनियम , 1962 
( 1962 का 50 ) को धारा 3 की माग ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, “ नमे उपयोग के अधिकार मा अर्जन करने के अपने प्राशय की 
घोषणा करती है । 

उक्त मनुसूची में वर्णित भूमि में हित ब कोई व्यक्ति, गजपत्र में यथा प्रकाशित इस अधिगूचना की प्रसियो जनता को उमसम्ध कर। दिए गाने की 
सारीख में 11दिन के भीतर उनमें आयोग के अधिकार का प्रजन या भूमि मे पाइपलाइन बिछाने में आपनि लिखित रूप में श्री कृपाल सिंह, भूमि अर्जन 
अधिकारी और मक्षम प्राधिकारी, इंदियन प्रायल कापोरेशन लिमिटे , कामा- भटिंडा पाइपलाइन परियोजना सैक्टर 37 - मी , एम मी पो 178 चंडीगत को पार 
सकेगा ।

$ .O . 835 : — Whereas it appears to the Central Government that it is necessary in the 
public interest that for the transport of Petroleum from Kandla in the State of Gujarat to Bhatinda in tho 
State ofPunjab , via Rajasthan and Haryana , Pipelines should be laid by thc Indian Oil Corporation Limited ; 

And whereas it appears that for the purpose of laying such pipelines it is necessary to acquire the right 
of user in the land described in the Schedule annexed to this notification ; 

Now , therefore , in excrcise of the powers conferred by sub - section ( 1 ) of section 3 of the Petroleum and 
Minerals Pipelines (Acquisition of right of User in Land ) Act , 1962 (50 of 1962 ) the Central Government 
hereby declares its intention to acquire the right of user therein , 

Any person interested in the land described in the said schedule may within 21 days from the date on 
which tho copies of this notification as published in the Gazette of India , are made available to the general 
public, object in writing to the acquisition of the right of user therein or laying of the pipeline under the land 
to Shri Kirpal Singh Land Acquisition Officer and Competent Authority , Indian Oil Corporation Ltd ., 
Kandla -Bhatinda Pipeline Project, Section 37- C SCO 178 Chandigarh .

[ R. 31015/ 2/ 90- ORI ] 


का०मा० 838 - केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है कि लोक हित में ऐसा करना पावश्यक है कि गुजरात राज्य में कांग्ला से पंजाब राज्य में मटिण्डा सक राजस्थान और 
हरियाणा से होकर पैट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिए इंडियन प्रायल कार्पोरेशन लिमिटेड द्वारा पाइपलाइन बिछाई जाए ; 

पीर ऐसा प्रतीत होता है कि उक्त पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजनों के लिए इस पघिसूचना से उपाबद्ध प्रमुसूची में वणित भूमि के उपयोग के अधिकार का मर्जन करना 
भावश्यक है ; अतः, प्रब, केन्द्रीय सरकार, पैट्रोलियम पार खनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन ) अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 
की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, उनमें उपयोग के अधिकार का प्रर्जन करने के अपने प्राशय की घोषणा करती है । 

उक्त अनुसूची में वर्णित भूमि में हित बस कोई व्यक्ति, राजपत्र में यक्षा प्रकाशित इस अधिसूचना की प्रतिया जनता को उपलब्ध करा दिए जाने की तारीख से 21 विन 
के भीतर सममें उपयोग के अधिकार का प्रणम या भूमि में पाइपलाइनबिछाने में पापसि लिखित रूप में श्री कृपाल सिंह, भूमि पर्जन अधिकारी और सक्षम प्राधिकारी , इंडियन 
पायल कार्पोरेशन लिमिटेड, कारला-मटिंग पाइपलाइन परियोजना , मैक्टर 37- सी , एस सी मो 178 चंडीगढ़ को कर सकेगा ।

कुलदीप सिह, अपर सचिव 


S . O . 836 . - Whereas it appears to the Central Government that it is necessary in the public 
interest that for the transport of Petroleum from Kandla in the State ofGujarat to Bhatinda in the State 
of Punjab ,via Rajasthan and Haryana, Pipelines should be laid by the Indian Oil Corporation Limited ; 

And whereas it appears that for the purpose of laying such pipelines it is necessary to acquire the 
right of user in the land described in the Schedule annexed to this notification ; 

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by sub - section (1) of section 3 of the Petroleum 
and Minerals pipelines (Acquisition of Right of User in Land ) Act, 1962 (50 of 1962 ), the Central Govern 
ment hereby declares its intention to acquire the right of user thcrein ; 

Any person interested in the land described in the said schedule may within 21 days from the date on 
which the copies of this notification , as published in the Gazette of India , are made available to the general 
public object in writing to the acquisition of the right of user therein or laying of the pipeline under the land 
to Shri Kripal Singh Land Acquisition Officer and Competent Authority Indian Oil Corporation Ltd . 
Kandla - Bhatinda Pipeline Project Sector 37 - C SCO 178 Chandigarh .

AWARD 
The dispute between ny ( . Rajan , Casual Piece Rate 
Worker , Shornur and the Divis. onal Railway Manager , Pal 
ghat in connection with the termination of service of the 
said Rajan with effect from 26 -11- 1987 was rcferred to this 
Court for adjudication by the Central Government under 
Section 10 ( 1 ) ( c ) of the Irrlustsial Disputes Act by the Order 
No . L -41012 /49 , 88 -DJI ( B ) ( lad 12 . 5 - 1989. On receipt 
of the reference in this Court both the workman and the 
management viz ., the Railways eniered appearance and filer 
statements setting forth their stand ir the dispute puisuant 
to the notice issued from this Court. 

2 . The brief averments in the statement filed by the 
workman are as follows : - - 
This workman was wcil.ing in the Parcel Unit of the 

Shornur Railway Station from 27 - 5 - 1985. He was 
engaged for loading and unloading the parcels 
arriving at thai station . He is paid wages at the 
rate of Rs. 5 per inetric ton . His wages are paid 
from the fund of the Railway . He worked under 
the control and supervision of the Railways . Thus 
he is a workman under the Railways as defined in 
the Industrial Disputes Act. But on 26 - 11 - 1987 he 
was discharged from service without assigning any 
reason whatsoev : r. Since this workman being a 
permanent empigi ne uider the Railways, this 
action of the Railways terminating his service is 
illegal and unjust. Even if the Railways wants 
to terminate h s Survice that can be done chly 
after giving his wukiran notice pay and compen 
sation as provided in the Industrial Disputes Act. 
Hence this action of the Railways is illegal. There 
fore an award may be passed setting aside this 
order and directing the Railways to reinstate this 
workman with back wages, 


6 . Point No. 1. – The issue referred for adjudication is 
the legality or otherwise oi the termination of service of 
a workman by the Railways on 26 - 11 - 1987. The case of the 
workman is that from 27 - 5 - 1985 onwards he was working as 
a Parcel Porter in the parcel unit of the Shornur Railway 
Station and while so on 20 - 11- 1987 his service was termi 
nated by the Railways witísut assigning any reason what 
soever . Now it is this action of the Railways that is called 
in question by the workman . According to him he has 
worked continuously for 2 years from 27 - 5 - 1985 and he 
was paid wages at the ralj of Rs. 5 per metric ton viz ., 
on piece rate basis and that this wage is paid from the 
Railway funds and that he worked as a Porter under the 
control and supervision Vi The Railways. Thus according 
to him he is . a permanent emploves under the Railways and 
hence the action of the Railways dispensing with his service 
on 26 -11- 1987 is illegal and unjust. Now the case of the 
Railways is that he is not a permanent employee under the 
Railways and that he worked as a Porter only as a casual 
labourer . According to the Railways the loading and un 
loading of parcels in the parul unit of the Shornur Junction 
are done by the Railways Licence Porters and on certain 
occasions there will be a huge increase in incoming parcels 
which cannot be handled by the Licence Porters and on 
sich occasions only the Railway has availed of the service 
of outsiders like the woman and that the workman woiked 
in that parcel unit only as a casual labourer . 


7 . Any way the short question is whether the workman 
is under the employment vi the Railways . As stated earlier 
it is the admitted case that the workman has worked in the 
jarcel unit for wages at the rate of Rs. 5 per metric ton . 
But simply because a persoil has worked under anotlier that 
will not that by itself make him an employee and a person 
becomes an emplovce under anciher only if the employer has 
control and supervision over the employee . Here it is more 
or less the admitted case that the Railways has absolutely 
no control over this worknian even when he is working and 
that the workman is not supposed to attend for work every 
dav. Or in other words whenever there is increase in in 
raming parcel , the Railway will avail the service of cutsiders 
and any outeirler can coine and load and unload the parcels 
and they will be paid on piece rate basis at the rate of 
Ps. 5 per metric ton . Or to be more precise the Railway 
Carnot even insist for the presence on every day or any 
particular day . So also , they are not supposed to work till 
a particular time and that they can go at any time. The 
cnly condition is that if they handle one ton load of parcel

3. The case of the workman in brief is that the workmen were appointed against existing vacant 
posts in subordinate cadre in the category of permanent employee as defined in para 503 of the Sastri Award 
The work performed by the workmen concerned is of permament nature and they have been stopped from 
attending to their duties not for the reason of want of work . The management engaged fresh hands in 
place of the workmen for attending the same work after discontinuing the employment of the workmen con 
cerned in this dispute . Management has caused deliberate breaks in the employment of the workmen 
with an ulterior motive to deprive them of the benefits of permanency and protection available under 
the Industrial Disputes Act. 


4 . They have been discontinued from the employment without any reason notioe , notice pay or 
compensation despite the directives of the said award and contrary to the provisions of the Industrial 
Disputes Act, 1947 . 


5 . The Bank management has issued circular, instruccions o its officers regarding engaging person 
in the subordinate cadre for few days and to discontinue their employment on expiry of 75/90 / 200 days 
and they are not to be given employment there after. Officers have also been asked to engage fresh hands 
after discontinuing such workmen from working. This is unfair labour practice . They are in continuous 
service of the Bank within the meaning of Sec . 25B of the I. D . Act as the cessation of work is not due to 
any fault on the part of the workmen concerned . Despite their representations and conciliation proceeding 
the workmen have not been given redress. The workmen pray that it be held that the action of the manage 
ment in terminating /discontinuing the services of the workmen is illegal, unjustificd and void and they be 
granted relief of benefit of continuity of service , reinstatement in service with full back wages and all other 
benefits to which the workmen would have been entitled to for the wrongful termination of their employment. 


6 . According to the management, the workmen are not " workmen " within the meaning of Seo . 2 (5 ) 
of the I.D . Act. They never worked continuously in one year for 240 days excludning Sunc ays and holiday , 

rated workmen . Their work commenced with the beginning of the day and ended with 
the close of the working hours. The provisions of Sec . 25B of the I. D . Act are not applica ble. Manage 
ment has venied that there were existin , posts in the subordinate cadre in the category of perinanent 
employee as defined in para 508 of the Sastri Award . They have denied than deliberate break was caused 
by the managemen . All other adverse allegations were denied by the manavement. Workmen have 
worked as follows :--

under Section 25- B of the I. D . Act ; and

Section 25 - H of tho . D . Act ? 


This itself revoals of the fact that there was permanent 
Vacancy through out the period the workmen wero in emp 
laymont and they were not absorbed because they could 
not stand interview which follows by itself that other per

12. It is needless to say that these employees were work 
men well within the mcaning of Section 2 (6 ) of tho I D . 
Act and they were retrenched from service as defined in 
Section 2 (00 ) of the I. D . Act . Even Tikka Mazdoors 
have been treated as workmen within the definition of I , D . 
Act. ( See H . D . Singh Vs. Reserve Bank of India and 
othes- 1985 SSC (L & S ) p . 975 ; Mohan Lal Vs. Manage 
ment of M / s. Bharat Electronic Ltd . - - AIR 1981 SC 18 ; 
Managemçnt of Karnatak State Roadways Transport Cor

Punjab Land Development & Reclamation Corporation Ltd . 
Chandigarh Vs. Presiding Officer , Labour Court & others 
decided by Supreme Court vide Judgment dated 4 -5 - 1990 ). 
Obviously termination of services of these workmen are 
not within any exceptions of the provisions of law .

16 . Next question which is material to be decided in 
this case is whether the service rendered by the workman 
for a period of 240 days was continuous servico . The 
definition of the expression " continuous servico" is preferred 
with the words " for the purpose of this Chapter" which 
means that the fucior of continuity or interruption of 600 
vicc in terns of this definition is to be limited only for the 
purpose of this Chapter viz . for calculating qualifying ser 
vice and payment of compension under the provisions 
contained in this Chapter. The words " continuous servico " 
occur in Section 25 - B , 23 - V , 25 - F , 25-FF . 25- FFF in Chap 
ter VI give certain rights and advant: yes to the workman 
who have been in " continuous service" for specified period 
of time. The section further Jays down that the serviço 
shall be deemed to be continuous inspit of its intrruption 
On account of the reasons added therein and should remain 
uninterrupted. While dealing with Sub -section (1 ) related 
to continuous service for a period, it consists of two parts 
i.e . (i) uninterrupted service- - the use of the words " shall 
be said to be in continuous service " makes the part of the 


(YTT II 


(ii) ]

1487 


It has further been expressed 113 follon 


: 


definition exhaustive i.e. all interrupted service means con 
tinuous servicc , on account of the following reasons viz . 
(a ) sickness (b ) unauthorises lonve (c ) accident ( d ) strike 
which is not illegal ( c ) lock out and (f) cessation of work 
i.e . not due to any fault on the part of the workman shall 
be included in the continuouis service 


" 11 is for the workman to low by adducing iclcvant 

cvidence that he has been in continuous service 
for not less than one year under the employer who

period of twelve months, he has served for not 
ices than 240 days for maintaining his complaint 
that the provisions of S . 25 - F were not complied 
with ."

" Ihc expression which wc are required to construo is 

actually worked inder ihe employer . This ex 
pression , according to us, cunnot mean those days 
only when the worluan worked with hammer , 
sickle or pen , but must necessarily comprehend all 
those days during which he was in the employ 
mont of the employer and for which he had been 
paid wages either under cxpress or implied con 
tract of service or by compulsion of statute , stand 
jag orders etc . The learned counsel for the Ma 
nagement would urge that only those days which 
are mentioned in the Explanation to sectio n 25- B 
( 2 ) should be taken into account for the purpose of 
calculating the numher of days on which the work 
man had actually worked though he had cot so 
worked and no other days . We do not think that 
wc are entitled to so constrain the construction of 
the expression actually worked under the emplo 
yer is capable of comprchending the days during 
which the workmau was in employment and was 
paid wagcy and we see no impediment to su cons 
truc thc expression there is no reason why the ex 
prossion should be limited by the explanation. To 
give it any other meaning than what we have donc 
would bring the object of Section 25 - F very close 
to frustation . It is not necessary to give examples 
of how 25- F may be frustrated as they are too 
obvious to be stuted ."

20 . See also O . P . Malhotra " The Law of IndustȚial Dis

The principles of statutory construction are well set 

tled . Words occurring in statures of liberal import 
such as social welfare legislation and Human Rights 
logisation aro not to be put in procrustean beds or 
shrunk to Lilipution Jimçnsions. In construing 
those legislations the imposture of literal construc 
tion must be avoided and the prodigality of its mis 
application must be recognised and reduced . 
Judges ought to be more concerned with the 
colour , the content and the context of such sta 
tutcs (we have borrowed the words from Lord Wilber 
force s opinion in Prenn v . Simmonds, (6 ) ( 1971 
( 3 ) A . E . R . 237 ) . In the same opinion Lord Wil 
berforce pointed out that law is not to bo loft bo 
hind in some island of literal interpretation but is 
to enquire beyond tho language, unisolated from the 
matrix of facts in which they are set ; the law is 
not to be interpreted purely on the internal Linguis 
tic considerations. In one of the casca cited hefoio 
us, that is Surendra Kumar Verma V . Central Go 
vernment Industrie Tribunal-cun - Labour Court, 
( upra ) we had occasion to suy , " Semantic luxuries 
are misplaced in the interpretution of bread and 
butter statutcs. Welfare statutes niunt, of neces 
sity , receive a broad interpretation, Where legisla 
tion is designed to give relief against certain kinds 
of mischicf, the Court is not to make inroads by 
muking etymological executions." 


" There could therefore , bu no scope for argument that 

paid holidays are to be included in " actually work 
ed " , certain denied actual working days, Only 
those days which are provided in the cyplanation 
could be included in calculating 240 days in addi 
tion to the actual working duys. The Explana 
tion to Section 25 - B has included in the expression 
“ the actually worked " , certain deened actual wor 
king days. Only those days which are provided 
in the explanation could be included in calculating 
240 days in addition to the actual working days. 
The meaning of the words " actually worked " can 
not he enlarge beyond what is contained in the 
Explanation ,"

- Reference No. 159189 
Employers in relation to the Management of Basta 
colla Area of Ms. BCCL . 

AND 
Their Workmen , 

Petition of Compromise 
The humble petition on behalf of the parties to the 
above reference most respectfully shewth . 

1. That the above dispute has been amicably settled 
between the parties on the following terms : 

Terms of Settlement 
a ) That the concerned workman Sri Bharat 

Nishad will be given employment within 30 
days from the date of his reporting for his 
duty on some non - ensitive job maintaining 

his original clerical grade . 
(b ) That after observing his sincere and honest 

conduct for a period of one year his case 
will be reviewed for his reinstate with con 
tinuity of service treating the period of idle 
11655 from the date of his dicmis al till his 

resumption duty as leave without wages . 
(c ) That the concerned workman will not be 

entitled to back wages and benefits for the 
period from the date of his dismissal till the 
resumption of his duty and will not claim 

for the same subsequently . 
3 . That in view of the aforesaid Settlement, there 
remains nothing to be adjudicated . 

Under the facts and circumstances stated above the 
Hon ble Tribunal will be graciously plcased to accept 
the Settlement as fair and proper and be pleased to 
pass the Award in terms of the Settlement. 
For the workman 

For the Employers.

INDUSTRY . Coal.

AWARD 
The present reference arises out of Order No. L 
20012137189 - I. R . (Coal. I), dated , New Delhi, the 
15th November, 1989 passed by the Central Govern 
ment in respect of an industrial dispute between the 
parties mentioned above. The subject matter of the 
dispute has been specified in the Schedule to the said 
order and the said schedule runs as follows : 
“ Whether the action of the management of Basta 

colla Area No . IX of M /s . Bharat Coking 
Coal Ltd . in dismissing Shri Bharat Nishad . 
Sales Assistant bide their letter No . BCCL ) 
IX GM |871- 81949 dated 8 | 12 June , 1987 
is justified ? If not, to what relief the 

workman concerres is entitled : " 
2 . The dispute has been settled out of Court . A 
memorandum of settlement has been filed in Court I 
have gone througli the terms of settlement and I find 
the quite fair and reasonable . There is no reason why 
an Award should not be made on the basis of terms 
and conditions laid down in the memorandum of 
settlement. I accept it and make an award accord 
ingly The memorandum of settlement shall form 
part of the Award .

ernment in respect of an Industria ) dispute between 
the parties mentioned above . The subject matter 
of the dispute has been specified in the Schedule to 
the said order and the said schedule runs as follows : 
“ Whether the action of the managenient of 

Bastacolla Area VO IX of Ms. Bharat

Prasad Singh , Weigh Bridge Clerk , vide 
their letter No. BCCLIX GM 87 : L - 81948 
dated 8 12 -6 - 1987 is justified ? If not, 
what relief the concerned workman cntitled 

to ?!

an award accordingly . The memorandum of settle 
tiert shall form part of the Award .
